### Q59. JEE Main 2021 (24 Feb Shift 1)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1990</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math> pressure, there are equal number of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> molecules and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cl</mi></math> atoms in the reaction mixture. The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub></math> for the reaction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></msub></math> under the above conditions is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>x</mi><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo></math> The value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math> is______(Rounded off to the nearest integer)

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **5**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>Cl</mi></math></p><p>Let mol of both of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cl</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi>Cl</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mi>Cl</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#8658;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math></p>

</details>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>Fe</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>Fe</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>(</mo><mi>RT</mi><mo>)</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>(</mo><mi>RT</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>(</mo><mi>RT</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>(</mo><mi>RT</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mn>3</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>(</mo><mi>RT</mi><msup><mo>)</mo><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>/</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><msup><mfenced><mi>RT</mi></mfenced><mi>&#916;ng</mi></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><msup><mfenced><mi>RT</mi></mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mn>3</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow></msup></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><msup><mfenced><mi>RT</mi></mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></msup></math></p>

</details>

### Q63. JEE Main 2020 (05 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `numerical` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

For a reaction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>X</mi><mo>+</mo><mi>Y</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>Z</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>mol</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>,</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>mol</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>mol</mi></math> of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">Z</mi></math> were taken in a <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> vessel and allowed to react. At equilibrium, the concentration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> was <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>0</mn><msup><mi>molL</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>. the equilibrium constant of the reaction is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mfrac><mi>x</mi><mn>15</mn></mfrac><mo>.</mo></math> The value of x is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>&#8230;</mo><mo>.</mo></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Value:** **16**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>            <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>+</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">z</mi></math></p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>1</mn><mi>mol</mi></math>  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>mol</mi><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>5</mn><mi>mol</mi></math></p><p>Since moles of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Z</mi></math> are increased at equilibrium therefore reaction goes in forward direction to attain the
equilibrium.</p><p><img height="219" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/a4e8cad9-9f41-4a9f-88bc-fa8a38dc8353-image.png" width="256" /></p>

</details>

### Q64. JEE Main 2020 (04 Sep Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

If the equilibrium constant for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup></math> and that of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>,</mo></math> the equilibrium constant for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></math> is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>/</mo><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>-</mo><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><mo>+</mo><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup><msubsup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi><mrow><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></mrow></msubsup></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>On adding Reaction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>1</mn><mi>st</mi></msup></math> and Reaction <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>2</mn><mi>nd</mi></msup></math> we get.</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi></msub><mo>(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>)</mo><mo>&#183;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi>eq</mi></msub><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>(</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>)</mo></math> </p>

</details>

### Q65. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

An open beaker of water in equilibrium with water vapour is in a sealed container. When a few grams of glucose are added to the beaker of water, the rate at which water molecules :

**Options:**

- **(A)** leaves the vapour increases
- **(B)** leaves the solution increases
- **(C)** leaves the solution decreases
- **(D)** leaves the vapour decreases

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) leaves the vapour increases**

**Detailed Solution:**

The vapour pressure of solution will be less than vapour pressure of pure solvent, so some vapour molecules will get condensed to maintain new equilibrium.

</details>

### Q66. JEE Main 2020 (02 Sep Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>For the following Assertion and Reason, the correct option is</p><p>Assertion (A) : When <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>Cu</mi></math> (II) and sulphide ions are mixed, they react together extremely quickly to give a solid.</p><p>Reason (R) : The equilibrium constant of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>Cu</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>+</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced><mi>aq</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup><mfenced><mi>aq</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi>Cus</mi><mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced></math> is high because the solubility product is low.</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** (A) is false and (R) is true.
- **(B)** Both (A) and (R) are false.
- **(C)** Both (A) and (R) are true but (R) is not the explanation for (A).
- **(D)** Both (A) and (R) are true and (R) is the explanation for (A).

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Both (A) and (R) are true but (R) is not the explanation for (A).**

**Detailed Solution:**

Rate of chemical reaction has nothing to do with value of equilibrium constant.

</details>

### Q68. JEE Main 2019 (12 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

In which one of the following equilibria, <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&#8800;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>?</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
- **(D)** <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></msub><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></math>
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">i</mi><mo>)</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>HI</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">I</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>
(ii) <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>
(iii) <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>
(iv) <math><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>RT</mtext></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">&#916;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow></msup><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mtext>RT</mtext></mrow></mfenced></math>
<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></mrow></msub><mo>&#62;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub></math>

</details>

### Q69. JEE Main 2019 (10 Apr Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

For the reaction,

<math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>+</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>3</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo></math>

<math><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>57.2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>1.7</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>16</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo></math>

Which of the following statements is incorrect?

**Options:**

- **(A)** The equilibrium constant is large suggestive of reaction going to completion and so, no catalyst is required.
- **(B)** The equilibrium will shift in forward direction as the pressure increases.
- **(C)** The equilibrium constant decreases as the temperature increases.
- **(D)** The addition of inert gas at constant volume will not affect the equilibrium constant.

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) The equilibrium constant is large suggestive of reaction going to completion and so, no catalyst is required.**

**Detailed Solution:**

In option <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&Delta;</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">n</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>-</mo></math>ve therefore, increase in pressure will bring reaction in forward direction.

In option <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C </mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo></math> as the reaction is exothermic therefore, increase in temperature will decrease the equilibrium constant.

In option <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D </mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>-</mo></math> Equilibrium constant changes only with temperature.

Hence, option <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal"> </mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> and <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> are correct. Therefore, option <math><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced></math> is incorrect choice.

</details>

### Q71. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Two solids dissociate as follows:

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi>atm</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">s</mi></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mfenced><mo>;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msub><mo>=</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mtext>&#8201;&#8201;</mtext><msup><mi>atm</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

The total pressure when both the solids dissociate simultaneously is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msqrt><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>+</mo><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>atm</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msqrt></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mfenced separators="|"><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msqrt></mfenced><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p><img height="134" src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/05fdc4f9-8d85-4218-911e-f8c0abd9c2aa-image.png" width="386" />

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></mrow></mfenced></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">k</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub></msub><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><msup><mrow><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo></mrow><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msqrt></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msqrt></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi>Total</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">E</mi></msub></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>1</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>)</mo><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msqrt><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msqrt></math></p>

</details>

### Q72. JEE Main 2019 (12 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

In a chemical reaction, <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mover><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mover><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> , the initial concentration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> was <math><mn>1.5</mn></math> times of the concentration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> , but the equilibrium concentrations of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math> were found to be equal. The equilibrium constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mfenced></math> for the chemical reaction is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>1</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>4</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>16</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math><mn>4</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mi>A      </mi><mo>+      </mo><mn>2</mn><mi>B      </mi><munder><mrow><mo>⇌</mo></mrow><mrow><mi> </mi></mrow></munder><mn>      2</mn><mtext>C</mtext><mo>    +    </mo><mtext>D</mtext></math>
Initial conc. of <math><mtext>[A] = a , [B] = 1.5a , [C] = 0 and [D] = 0</mtext></math>

At equilibrium <math><mtext>[A] = (a - x) , [B] = 1.5(a - 2x) , [C] = 2x and [D] = x</math></mtext>

At equilibrium <math><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1.5</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></math>

<math><mn>0.5</mn><mtext> </mtext><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>x</mi></math>

<math><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi>k</mi></mrow><mrow><mi>C</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1.5</mn><mi>a</mi><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>.</mo><mi>x</mi></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced><msup><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi>x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

</details>

### Q73. JEE Main 2019 (11 Jan Shift 1)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

Consider the reaction $\mathrm{N}_{2}(\mathrm{~g})+3 \mathrm{H}_{2}(\mathrm{~g}) \rightleftharpoons 2 \mathrm{NH}_{3}(\mathrm{~g})$

The equilibrium constant of the above reaction is $\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{P}}$. If pure ammonia is left to dissociate, the partial pressure of ammonia at equilibrium is given by (Assume that

$\mathrm{P}_{\mathrm{NH}_{3}}< < \mathrm{P}_{\text {total }}$ at equilibrium)

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\frac{3^{3 / 2} \mathrm{~K}_{\mathrm{P}}^{1 / 2} \mathrm{P}^{2}}{16}$
- **(B)** $\frac{\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{P}}^{1 / 2 \mathrm{P}^{2}}}{16}$
- **(C)** $\frac{\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{P}}^{1 / 2 \mathrm{P}^{2}}}{4}$
- **(D)** $\frac{3^{3 / 2} \mathrm{~K}_{\mathrm{P}}^{1 / 2} \mathrm{P}^{2}}{4}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $\frac{3^{3 / 2} \mathrm{~K}_{\mathrm{P}}^{1 / 2} \mathrm{P}^{2}}{16}$**

### Q74. JEE Main 2019 (10 Jan Shift 2)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `3`

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>.1</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>SH</mi></math> is introduced in <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>.0</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math> evacuated flask at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>327</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> . <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>30</mn><mo>%</mo></math> of the solid <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mi>SH</mi></math> is  decomposed to <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math> as gases. The <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub></math> of the reaction at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mn>327</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.082</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>atm</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>Molar</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>mass</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>of</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>32</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>Molar</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>mass</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>of</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>14</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>mol</mi><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></mrow></mfenced></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.242</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>atm</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.242</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>4</mn></mrow></msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msup><mi>atm</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8757;</mo><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>RT</mi></mfenced><mi>&#916;ng</mi></msup></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>RT</mi></mfenced><mi>&#916;ng</mi></msup></math>

(i)  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>NO</mi><mtext>&#8201;</mtext><mi>&#916;ng</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></math>

(ii)  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi><mn>4</mn></msub><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><msub><mi>Na</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>&#916;ng</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>RT</mi></mfenced><mn>1</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>24</mn><mo>.62</mo></mrow></mfenced><mn>1</mn></msup></math>

(iii)  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>+</mo><mn>3</mn><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi>NH</mi><mn>3</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi>&#916;ng</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></math>  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8756;</mo><mfrac><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi></msub><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></msub></mfrac><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mi>RT</mi></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><msup><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>24</mn><mo>.62</mo></mrow></mfenced><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>.659</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow></msup></math><math> <mrow> <mo>&#8771;</mo><mn>1.65</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup> <mrow> <mn>10</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mo>&#8722;</mo><mn>3</mn></mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

### Q76. JEE Main 2018 (16 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

For standardizing <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>NaOH</mi></math> solution, which of the following is used as a primary standard?

**Options:**

- **(A)** Sodium tetra borate
- **(B)** Ferrous Ammonium sulphate
- **(C)** Oxalic acid
- **(D)** Dil. HCl

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Oxalic acid**

**Detailed Solution:**

<blockquote><p>A <b>primary standard</b> in metrology is a standard that is sufficiently accurate such that it is not calibrated by or subordinate to other standards. Primary standards are defined via other quantities like length, mass and time. Primary standards are used to calibrate other standards referred to as working standards.</p><p>Oxalic acid is used as a primary standard for <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>NaOH</mi></math> standardization.</p></blockquote>

</details>

### Q77. JEE Main 2018 (15 Apr Shift 2 Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

At a certain temperature in a 5L vessel, 2 moles of carbon monoxide and 3 moles of chlorine were allowed to reach equilibrium according to the reaction, $\mathrm{CO}+\mathrm{Cl}_2 \rightleftharpoons \mathrm{COCl}_2$. At equilibrium, if one mole of $\mathrm{CO}$ is present then equilibrium constant $\left(K_{\mathrm{c}}\right)$ for the reaction is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** $2.5$
- **(B)** 4
- **(C)** 2
- **(D)** 3

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $2.5$**

**Detailed Solution:**

Initially 2 moles of $\mathrm{CO}$ are present.
At equilibrium, 1 mole of $\mathrm{CO}$ is present Hence, $2-1=1$ moles of $\mathrm{CO}$ has reacted. 1 mole of CO will react with 1 mole of $\mathrm{Cl}_2$ to form 1 mole of $\mathrm{COCl}_2$. $3-1=2$ moles of $\mathrm{Cl}_2$ remains at equilibrium The equilibrium constant
$$
K_{\mathrm{c}}=\frac{\left[\mathrm{COCl}_2\right]}{[\mathrm{CO}]\left[\mathrm{Cl}_2\right]}=\frac{\frac{1 \mathrm{~mol}}{5 \mathrm{~L}}}{\frac{1 \mathrm{~mol}}{5 \mathrm{~L}} \times \frac{2 \mathrm{~mol}}{5 \mathrm{~L}}}=2.5
$$

</details>

### Q78. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

An aqueous solution contains  <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>20</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>HCl</mi></math>. If the equilibrium constant for the formation of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>HS</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math> from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mn>2</mn></msub><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi></math> is <math><mn>1.0</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>7</mn></mrow></msup></math> and that of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> from <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi>HS</mi><mo>-</mo></msup></math> ions is <math><mn>1.2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>13</mn></mrow></msup></math>, then, the concentration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>-</mo></mrow></msup></math> ions in the aqueous solution is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>19</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(B)** <math><mn>5</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>8</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(C)** <math><mn>3</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>20</mn></mrow></msup></math>
- **(D)** <math><mn>6</mn><mo>×</mo><msup><mrow><mn>10</mn></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>21</mn></mrow></msup></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

### Q79. JEE Main 2018 (08 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Which of the following lines correctly show the temperature dependence of equilibrium constant <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>,</mo></math> for an exothermic reaction?
<img src="https://cdn-question-pool.getmarks.app/pyq/jee_main/0574e5f3-5aba-4164-92c9-b606092c19c1-image.png" style="width: 259px; height: 235px;" />

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mo>&#38;</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<p>The Arrhenius equation in Logarithmic form:</p><p><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>In</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mi>RT</mi></mfrac><mo>+</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math></p><p>On comparing with <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>mx</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>,</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi></mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi></mfrac></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">c</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>ln</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></math>.</p><p>For an exothermic reaction, the value of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8710;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">H</mi><mo>&#60;</mo><mn>0</mn></math>, slope will be positive and the intercept can be either positive or negative.</p>

</details>

### Q80. JEE Main 2016 (10 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

<p>A solid <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi>XY</mi><mo>&#160;</mo></math>kept in an evacuated sealed container undergoes decomposition to form a mixture of gases <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>and</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></math> at temperature <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></math>.</p><p>The equilibrium pressure is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>bar</mi></math> in this vessel. <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub></math> for this reaction is?</p>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>25</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>10</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>25</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable><mtr><mtd><mi>XY</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8652;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">X</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">S</mi><mo>)</mo></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></mtd></mtr></mtable><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mo>&#160;&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mtable><mtr><mtd><mo>+</mo></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">Y</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd/><mtd><mo>(</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>)</mo></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi>eq</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mo>+</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub></math><math><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>

    <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>&#8658;</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mfenced separators="|"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></msub></mfenced><mo>.</mo><mo>(</mo><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">y</mi></msub><mo>)</mo></math>

     <math><mo>=</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>5</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>25</mn></math>

</details>

### Q81. JEE Main 2016 (03 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The equilibrium constant at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>298</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> for a reaction <math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn></math>. If the initial concentration of all the four species were<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math> each, then the equilibrium concentration of <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> (in mol <math><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>) will be:

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>818</mn></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>182</mn></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>182</mn></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>818</mn></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>818</mn></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

K at <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>298</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> for the reaction
<math><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>100</mn></math>.

Given: The initial concentration of all four species is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></math>.
<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable><mtr><mtd/><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>+</mo></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8652;</mo></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>+</mo></mtd><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi mathvariant="normal">t</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mn>1</mn></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mi>at</mi><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>equilbrium</mi></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>

<math><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>100</mn></math>
<math> <mo>&#8658;</mo> </math> <math><mfrac><mrow><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfenced></mrow><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>10</mn></math>
<math> <mo>&#8658;</mo> </math> <math><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>9</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>11</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>0.818</mn></math>
<math> <mo>&#8658;</mo> </math> <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">D</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">x</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.818</mn><mo>=</mo><mn>1.818</mn></math>

</details>

### Q82. JEE Main 2015 (10 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

Gaseous <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> dissociates into gaseous <math><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></math> according to the reaction <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>&#8652;</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></mfenced></math>
At <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>300</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></math> and <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math> pressure, the degree of dissociation of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> is <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>0</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>2</mn></math>. If one mole of <math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></math> gas is contained in a vessel, then the density of the equilibrium mixture is<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>:</mo></math>

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>56</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>4</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>56</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>6</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>22</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>3</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>11</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>/</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math><mtable><mtr><mtd><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn></mrow></msub></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8652;</mo></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">N</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">O</mi></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></msub></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mfenced separators="|"><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced></mtd><mtd></mtd><mtd><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>

Total moles at equilibrium <math><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>+</mo><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1.2</mn></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></math> for equilibrium mixture <math> <mo>=</mo><mfrac> <mrow> <mn>0.8</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>92</mn><mo>+</mo><mn>0.4</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>46</mn></mrow> <mrow> <mn>1.2</mn></mrow>&#160;&#160;</mfrac></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mo>=</mo><mo>&#160;</mo><mn>76</mn><mo>.</mo><mn>67</mn><mo>&#160;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mo>/</mo><mi>mol</mi></math>

<math><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">d</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">r</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi></mrow></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">P</mi><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">M</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">a</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">v</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi></mrow></msub></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">T</mi></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>76.67</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>0.082</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>300</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>76.67</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>24.6</mn></mrow></mfrac></math>

<math><mo>=</mo><mn>3.11</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">g</mi><msup><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">L</mi></mrow><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msup></math>

</details>

### Q83. JEE Main 2015 (04 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

The standard Gibbs energy change at 300 K for the reaction <math><mn>2</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><mo>&#8652;</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>+</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></math> is 2494.2 J. At a given time, the composition of the reaction mixture is <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></math> and <math><mfenced close="]" open="[" separators="|"><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></mfenced><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn></mrow><mrow><mn>2</mn></mrow></mfrac></math> . The reaction proceeds in the: <math><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">R</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>8.314</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">J</mi></mrow><mo>/</mo><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">m</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">o</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">l</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mo>,</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">&#160;</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">e</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2.718</mn><mo>]&#160;</mo></mrow></mrow></math> {Given antilog (-0.44)=0.36}

**Options:**

- **(A)** Reverse direction because  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(B)** Forward direction because  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(C)** Reverse direction because <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math>
- **(D)** Forward direction because  <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&lt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) Reverse direction because <math><mi mathvariant="normal">Q</mi><mo>&gt;</mo><msub><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi></mrow><mrow><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi></mrow></msub></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <mo>&#916;</mo> <msup> <mtext>G</mtext> <mo>&#8728;</mo> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <msub> <mtext>RT ln K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>2494.2</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>8.314</mtext> <mo>&#215;</mo> <msub> <mtext>300 ln K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>2494.2</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>8.314</mtext> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>300</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <msub> <mtext>2.303 log K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mtext>2494.2</mtext> </mrow> <mrow> <mtext>2.303</mtext> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>300</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mtext>8.314</mtext> </mrow> </mfrac> <mo>=</mo> <mo>-</mo> <mtext>0.44</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>log K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>log K</mtext><mtext>C</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mtext>0.44</mtext></math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.36</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>Q</mtext><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">B</mi><mo>]</mo><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">C</mi><mo>]</mo></mrow><mrow><mo>[</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">A</mi><msup><mo>]</mo><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>2</mn><mo>&#215;</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mrow><msup><mfenced><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mn>4</mn></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtext>Q</mtext><mo>&#62;</mo><msub><mtext>K</mtext><mtext>C</mtext></msub></math>, reaction will shift in reverse direction.

</details>

### Q84. JEE Main 2014 (19 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

For the decomposition of the compound, represented as

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>COONH</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#8652;</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>CO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

the <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mi mathvariant="normal">K</mi><mi mathvariant="normal">p</mi></msub><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo>.9</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>5</mn></mrow></msup><msup><mo>atm</mo><mn>3</mn></msup></math>. If the reaction is started with 1 mole of the compound, the total pressure at equilibrium would be :

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>7</mn><mo>.66</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>atm</mo></math>
- **(B)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>38</mn><mo>.8</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>atm</mo></math>
- **(C)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>.82</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>atm</mo></math>
- **(D)** <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>1</mn><mo>.94</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><msup><mn>10</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>&#160;</mo><mi>atm</mi></math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(C) <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mn>5</mn><mo>.82</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>1</mn><msup><mn>0</mn><mrow><mo>-</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow></msup><mo>atm</mo></math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

In case of heterogeneous equilibrium. The achive mass of solid is unit so not consider in the expression of <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>p</mtext> </msub> <mtext>&#8196;</mtext><mtext>and</mtext><mtext>&#8196;</mtext><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>c</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <msub> <mtext>COONH</mtext> <mn>4</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>s</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>&#8652;</mo> <mn>2</mn> <msub> <mtext>NH</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> <mo>+</mo> <msub> <mtext>CO</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>g</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> </math>

At equilibrium <math> <mrow> <mo>&#8658;</mo> </mrow> </math> 2 p and   p  will be partial pressures of ammonia and carbon dioxide gas respectively.

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mtext>&#8196;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#8196;</mtext><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>NH</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>CO</mtext></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced><mtext>&#8196;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#8196;</mtext><msup> <mrow> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mrow> <mtext>2P</mtext></mrow> </mfenced></mrow> <mtext>2</mtext> </msup> <mfenced close="]" open="["> <mtext>P</mtext> </mfenced><mtext>&#8196;</mtext><mtext>=</mtext><mtext>&#8196;</mtext><msup> <mrow> <mtext>4P</mtext></mrow> <mtext>3</mtext> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mn>4</mn> <msup> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#183;</mo> <mn>9</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <msup> <mtext>P</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>0</mn> <mo>&#183;</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>5</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> <mo>=</mo> <mn>7</mn> <mo>&#183;</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </msup> <msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> <mn>3</mn> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mroot> <mrow> <mn>7</mn> <mo>&#183;</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>6</mn> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> </mrow> </mroot> <mo>=</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#183;</mo> <mn>9</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow> <mi>&#931;</mi> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>P</mtext> <mtext>Total</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mtext>equilibrium</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <mn>2</mn> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>+</mo> <mtext>P</mtext> <mo>=</mo> <mn>3</mn> <mtext>P</mtext> </mrow> </math>

=<math> <mrow> <mn>3</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <mo>&#183;</mo> <mn>9</mn> <mn>4</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

=<math> <mrow> <mn>5</mn> <mo>&#183;</mo> <mn>8</mn> <mn>2</mn> <mo>&#215;</mo> <mn>1</mn> <msup> <mn>0</mn> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </msup> <mtext>atm</mtext> </mrow> </math>

</details>

### Q85. JEE Main 2014 (09 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `2`

At a certain temperature, only 50% HI is dissociated into H<sub>2</sub> and I<sub>2</sub> at equilibrium. The equilibrium constant is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** 0.5
- **(B)** 4.0
- **(C)** 1.0
- **(D)** 0.25

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) 0.25**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mtable><mtr><mtd/><mtd><mn>2</mn><mtext>H</mtext><mtext>I</mtext><mfenced><mtext>g</mtext></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>&#8652;</mo></mtd><mtd><msub><mtext>H</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mtext>g</mtext></mfenced></mtd><mtd><mo>+</mo></mtd><mtd><msub><mtext>I</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub><mfenced><mtext>g</mtext></mfenced></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mtext>Moles</mtext></mtd><mtd><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd><mtext>Concentration</mtext></mtd><mtd><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mtext>V</mtext></mfrac></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mtext>2V</mtext></mfrac></mtd><mtd/><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mtext>2V</mtext></mfrac></mtd></mtr></mtable></math>

<math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><msub><mtext>K</mtext><mtext>C</mtext></msub><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]"><msub><mtext>H</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]"><msub><mtext>I</mtext><mn>2</mn></msub></mfenced></mrow><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mrow><mtext>H</mtext><mtext>I</mtext></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mtext>2V</mtext></mfrac></mfenced><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mtext>2V</mtext></mfrac></mfenced></mrow><msup><mfenced open="[" close="]"><mfrac><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mrow><mtext>V</mtext></mfrac></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mfrac></math>

       <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mfenced><mtable><mtr><mtd/><mtd><mo>&#8756;</mo><mi>&#945;</mi></mtd><mtd><mo>=</mo></mtd><mtd><mtext>0.50</mtext></mtd></mtr><mtr><mtd/><mtd><mfrac><mi>&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></mfrac></mtd><mtd><mo>=</mo></mtd><mtd><mtext>0.25</mtext></mtd></mtr></mtable></mfenced></math>

       <math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><mi mathvariant="normal">=</mi><mfrac><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mrow><mn>4</mn><msup><mfenced><mrow><mn>1</mn><mo>-</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">&#945;</mi></mrow></mfenced><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mrow><mn>0</mn><mo>.50</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.50</mo></mrow><mrow><mn>4</mn><mo>&#215;0.50</mo><mo>&#215;</mo><mn>0</mn><mo>.50</mo></mrow></mfrac><mo>=</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mn>4</mn></mfrac></math>

       <math> <mrow><msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> </mrow> <mo>=</mo> <mtext>0.25</mtext></math>

</details>

### Q86. JEE Main 2014 (06 Apr)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

For the reaction <math> <mrow> <msub> <mrow> <mtext>SO</mtext></mrow> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>+</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <msub> <mtext>O</mtext> <mn>2</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced><mo>⇌</mo><msub> <mrow> <mtext>SO</mtext></mrow> <mn>3</mn> </msub> <mfenced> <mtext>g</mtext> </mfenced></mrow> </math> , if <math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>RT</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mi>x</mi> </msup> </mrow> </math> where the symbols have usual meaning then the value of <math><mi>x</mi></math> is:
(assuming ideality)

**Options:**

- **(A)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>
- **(B)** <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(C)** <math> <mrow> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>
- **(D)** <math> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> </math>

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(B) <math> <mrow> <mo>-</mo> <mfrac> <mrow> <mn>1</mn> </mrow> <mrow> <mn>2</mn> </mrow> </mfrac> </mrow> </math>**

**Detailed Solution:**

<math> <mrow> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>P</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo> <msub> <mtext>K</mtext> <mtext>C</mtext> </msub> <msup> <mrow> <mfenced close=")" open="(" separators=","> <mrow> <mtext>RT</mtext> </mrow> </mfenced> </mrow> <mrow> <mo>Δ</mo> <msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <mtext>g</mtext> </msub> </mrow> </msup> </mrow> </math>

<math> <mrow>  <msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>SO</mtext></mrow>   <mn>2</mn>  </msub>  <mo stretchy='false'>(</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mo stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo>+</mo><mfrac>   <mn>1</mn>   <mn>2</mn>  </mfrac>  <msub>   <mtext>O</mtext>   <mn>2</mn>  </msub>  <mo stretchy='false'>(</mo><mtext>g</mtext><mo stretchy='false'>)</mo><mo>&#x21CC;</mo><msub>   <mrow>    <mtext>SO</mtext></mrow>   <mn>3</mn>  </msub>  <mfenced>   <mtext>g</mtext>  </mfenced></mrow></math>

<math> <mtext>Δ</mtext><msub> <mtext>n</mtext> <mtext>g</mtext> </msub> <mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>−</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>1</mn><mo>+</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> <mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><mo>-</mo><mfrac> <mn>1</mn> <mn>2</mn> </mfrac> </math>

</details>

### Q87. JEE Main 2013 (25 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

The ratio $\frac{K_p}{K_c}$ for the reaction
$\mathrm{CO}(g)+\frac{1}{2} O_2(g) \rightleftharpoons C O_2(g)$ is:

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\frac{1}{\sqrt{R T}}$
- **(B)** $(R T)^{1 / 2}$
- **(C)** $\mathrm{RT}$
- **(D)** $1$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(A) $\frac{1}{\sqrt{R T}}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

$\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{P}}=\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{C}}(\mathrm{RT})^{\Delta \mathrm{n}_{\mathrm{g}}}$
For the reaction
$\begin{aligned}
& \mathrm{CO}(\mathrm{g})+\frac{1}{2} \mathrm{O}_2(g) \leftrightharpoons \mathrm{CO}_2(\mathrm{~g}) \\
& \Delta n_g=1-\left(1+\frac{1}{2}\right)=-\frac{1}{2}
\end{aligned}$
$\therefore K_{\mathrm{P}}=\frac{\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{C}}}{\sqrt{\mathrm{RT}}} ; \quad \frac{\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{P}}}{\mathrm{K}_{\mathrm{C}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{R T}}$

</details>

### Q88. JEE Main 2013 (23 Apr Online)

*Type:* `singleCorrect` | *Difficulty Level:* `1`

(1) $\mathrm{N}_2(\mathrm{~g})+3 \mathrm{H}_2(\mathrm{~g}) \rightleftharpoons 2 \mathrm{NH}_3(\mathrm{~g}), \mathrm{K}_1$
(2) $\mathrm{N}_2(\mathrm{~g})+\mathrm{O}_2(\mathrm{~g}) \rightleftharpoons 2 \mathrm{NO}(\mathrm{g}), \mathrm{K}_2$
(3) $\mathrm{H}_2(\mathrm{~g})+\frac{1}{2} \mathrm{O}_2(\mathrm{~g}) \rightleftharpoons \mathrm{H}_2 \mathrm{O}(\mathrm{g}), \mathrm{K}_3$
The equation for the equilibrium constant of the reaction
$$
2 \mathrm{NH}_3(\mathrm{~g})+\frac{5}{2} \mathrm{O}_2(\mathrm{~g}) \rightleftharpoons 2 \mathrm{NO}(\mathrm{g})+3 \mathrm{H}_2 \mathrm{O}(\mathrm{g}) \text {, }
$$
$\left(K_4\right)$ in terms of $\mathrm{K}_1, \mathrm{~K}_2$ and $\mathrm{K}_3$ is :

**Options:**

- **(A)** $\frac{\mathrm{K}_1 \cdot \mathrm{K}_2}{\mathrm{~K}_3}$
- **(B)** $\frac{\mathrm{K}_1 \cdot \mathrm{K}_3^2}{\mathrm{~K}_2}$
- **(C)** $\mathrm{K}_1 \mathrm{~K}_2 \mathrm{~K}_3$
- **(D)** $\frac{\mathrm{K}_2 \cdot \mathrm{K}_3^3}{\mathrm{~K}_1}$

<details>
<summary><b>View Answer & Solution</b></summary>

**Correct Option:** **(D) $\frac{\mathrm{K}_2 \cdot \mathrm{K}_3^3}{\mathrm{~K}_1}$**

**Detailed Solution:**

To calculate the value of $\mathrm{K}_4$ in the given equation we should apply :
eqn. (2) $+$ eqn. $(3) \times 3-$ eqn. (1)
hence $\mathrm{K}_4=\frac{\mathrm{K}_2 \mathrm{~K}_3^3}{\mathrm{~K}_1}$

</details>
